### 背景
plan_007 是 P0 任务：对 Llama 3.1 8B 所有条件做 full benchmark evaluation。
- Tasks: ARC-c, WinoGrande, MMLU, GSM8K（已有 dense + KL sparse 50% 结果）

- DDL: 2026-05-25（9天后）

已有结果（不需要重跑）：
- Dense: arc_challenge=0.5819, winogrande=0.7814, mmlu=0.6532, gsm8k=0.5004
- KL sparse 50% (s42): arc_challenge=0.3771, winogrande=0.6156, mmlu=0.2591, gsm8k=0.0121
- Mistral dense: arc_challenge=0.6024, winogrande=0.7869, mmlu=0.6244, gsm8k=0.3654

### 你需要做的

**Step 1: SSH 到 westd-34887 审查代码**（用 ssh_read_only）
```bash
# 查看 lm-eval 相关脚本
ls -la /root/distill_sparse_swiglu/src/lm_eval*.py

# 读取现有的 lm-eval 脚本

cat /root/distill_sparse_swiglu/src/lm_eval_teal.py

# 检查有没有 BCE/STE/training-free 的 lm-eval 适配
grep -r "bce\|ste\|teal\|wina\|training_free" /root/distill_sparse_swiglu/src/lm_eval*.py
```

ls -la /root/distill_sparse_swiglu/checkpoints/kl_sparsity30_s42_v3/predictor_kl_normalized.pt
ls -la /root/distill_sparse_swiglu/checkpoints/kl_sparsity70_s42/predictor_kl_normalized.pt
```

**Step 3: 确认哪些条件需要新脚本**
对于每个尚未跑过 downstream eval 的条件，判断：
- 现有哪个脚本可以直接跑（lm_eval_sparse.py? lm_eval_teal.py?）
- 需要什么参数（checkpoint 路径、sparsity target 等）

```
条件名 | 脚本 | 关键参数 | 预计时间 | checkpoint 路径

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"# lm-evaluation-harness integration for KL-distilled sparse Llama-3.1-8B (TEAL 50%).\\n# Loads base model + predictor checkpoint, computes TEAL static masks with per-layer\\n# mean normalization, patches MLP forwards, then runs lm-eval benchmarks.\\n# Input: predictor checkpoint, model path, task list\\n# Output: lm-eval results JSON in --output_path\\n# Dependencies: lm-eval >= 0.4, transformers, torch, datasets\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport importlib.util\\nimport json\\nimport os\\nimport sys\\n\\nimport torch\\nfrom transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer\\n\\nsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))\\nfrom data_utils import get_eval_dataset\\nfrom predictor import PredictorWrapper\\n\\n\\ndef _best_attn_impl():\\n    if importlib.util.find_spec(\\\"flash_attn\\\"):\\n        return \\\"flash_attention_2\\\"\\n    return \\\"sdpa\\\"\\n\\n\\ndef _global_topk_masks(layer_scores, sparsity_target):\\n    items = sorted(layer_scores.items())\\n    normalized = []\\n    for li, s in items:\\n        s_f = s.float()\\n        mu = s_f.mean()\\n        normalized.append((li, s_f / mu if mu > 1e-12 else s_f))\\n    all_s = torch.cat([s for _, s in normalized])\\n    num_keep = int(len(all_s) * (1.0 - sparsity_target))\\n    thr = torch.topk(all_s, num_keep).values[-1]\\n    return {li: (s >= thr).to(torch.bfloat16) for li, s in normalized}\\n\\n\\ndef teal_global_masks(wrapper, input_ids, sparsity_target, calib_batch_size=4):\\n    accum_scores = {}\\n    n_batches = 0\\n\\n    for start in range(0, input_ids.size(0), calib_batch_size):\\n        batch = input_ids[start:start + calib_batch_size]\\n        wrapper.forward_dense(batch, capture_intermediates=True)\\n        intermediates = wrapper.get_intermediates()\\n        layer_inputs = wrapper.get_layer_inputs()\\n        with torch.no_grad():\\n            for layer_idx in range(len(wrapper.predictors)):\\n                if layer_idx not in layer_inputs or layer_idx not in intermediates:\\n                    continue\\n                logits = wrapper.predictors[layer_idx](layer_inputs[layer_idx])\\n                hard_mask = (logits > 0).float()\\n                mag = intermediates[layer_idx].abs().float()\\n                scores = (mag * hard_mask).mean(dim=(0, 1))\\n                if layer_idx not in accum_scores:\\n                    accum_scores[layer_idx] = scores\\n                else:\\n                    accum_scores[layer_idx] = accum_scores[layer_idx] + scores\\n        n_batches += 1\\n\\n    layer_scores = {li: accum_scores[li] / n_batches for li in sorted(accum_scores)}\\n    return _global_topk_masks(layer_scores, sparsity_target)\\n\\n\\ndef apply_static_masks(model, global_masks, device):\\n    for layer_idx, layer in enumerate(model.model.layers):\\n        if layer_idx not in global_masks:\\n            continue\\n        mask = global_masks[layer_idx].to(device=device, dtype=layer.mlp.gate_proj.weight.dtype)\\n        mlp = layer.mlp\\n\\n        def make_masked_fwd(gp, up, dp, af, m):\\n            def fwd(x):\\n                intermediate = af(gp(x)) * up(x)\\n                return dp(intermediate * m.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0))\\n            return fwd\\n\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.45}}"}

--- Apply static masks permanently ---\\n    # First restore original forwards (PredictorWrapper patched them)\\n    for layer_idx, layer in enumerate(model.model.layers):\\n        mlp = layer.mlp\\n        gp, up, dp, af = mlp.gate_proj, mlp.up_proj, mlp.down_proj, mlp.act_fn\\n        def _make_orig(g, u, d, a):\\n            def fwd(x):\\n                return d(a(g(x)) * u(x))\\n            return fwd\\n        layer.mlp.forward = _make_orig(gp, up, dp, af)\\n\\n    apply_static_masks(model, global_masks, device)\\n    print(\\\"Static masks applied to model.\\\")\\n\\n    # {"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"# lm-evaluation-harness integration for KL-distilled sparse Llama-3.1-8B (TEAL 50%).\\n# Loads base model + predictor checkpoint, computes TEAL static masks with per-layer\\n# mean normalization, patches MLP forwards, then runs lm-eval benchmarks.\\n# Input: predictor checkpoint, model path, task list\\n# Output: lm-eval results JSON in --output_path\\n# Dependencies: lm-eval >= 0.4, transformers, torch, datasets\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport importlib.util\\nimport json\\nimport os\\nimport sys\\n\\nimport torch\\nfrom transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer\\n\\nsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))\\nfrom data_utils import get_eval_dataset\\nfrom predictor import PredictorWrapper\\n\\n\\ndef _best_attn_impl():\\n    if importlib.util.find_spec(\\\"flash_attn\\\"):\\n        return \\\"flash_attention_2\\\"\\n    return \\\"sdpa\\\"\\n\\n\\ndef _global_topk_masks(layer_scores, sparsity_target):\\n    items = sorted(layer_scores.items())\\n    normalized = []\\n    for li, s in items:\\n        s_f = s.float()\\n        mu = s_f.mean()\\n        normalized.append((li, s_f / mu if mu > 1e-12 else s_f))\\n    all_s = torch.cat([s for _, s in normalized])\\n    num_keep = int(len(all_s) * (1.0 - sparsity_target))\\n    thr = torch.topk(all_s, num_keep).values[-1]\\n    return {li: (s >= thr).to(torch.bfloat16) for li, s in normalized}\\n\\n\\ndef teal_global_masks(wrapper, input_ids, sparsity_target, calib_batch_size=4):\\n    accum_scores = {}\\n    n_batches = 0\\n\\n    for start in range(0, input_ids.size(0), calib_batch_size):\\n        batch = input_ids[start:start + calib_batch_size]\\n        wrapper.forward_dense(batch, capture_intermediates=True)\\n        intermediates = wrapper.get_intermediates()\\n        layer_inputs = wrapper.get_layer_inputs()\\n        with torch.no_grad():\\n            for layer_idx in range(len(wrapper.predictors)):\\n                if layer_idx not in layer_inputs or layer_idx not in intermediates:\\n                    continue\\n                logits = wrapper.predictors[layer_idx](layer_inputs[layer_idx])\\n                hard_mask = (logits > 0).float()\\n                mag = intermediates[layer_idx].abs().float()\\n                scores = (mag * hard_mask).mean(dim=(0, 1))\\n                if layer_idx not in accum_scores:\\n                    accum_scores[layer_idx] = scores\\n                else:\\n                    accum_scores[layer_idx] = accum_scores[layer_idx] + scores\\n        n_batches += 1\\n\\n    layer_scores = {li: accum_scores[li] / n_batches for li in sorted(accum_scores)}\\n    return _global_topk_masks(layer_scores, sparsity_target)\\n\\n\\ndef apply_static_masks(model, global_masks, device):\\n    for layer_idx, layer in enumerate(model.model.layers):\\n        if layer_idx not in global_masks:\\n            continue\\n        mask = global_masks[layer_idx].to(device=device, dtype=layer.mlp.gate_proj.weight.dtype)\\n        mlp = layer.mlp\\n\\n        def make_masked_fwd(gp, up, dp, af, m):\\n            def fwd(x):\\n                intermediate = af(gp(x)) * up(x)\\n                return dp(intermediate * m.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0))\\n            return fwd\\n\\n        layer.mlp.forward = make_masked_fwd(\\n            mlp.gate_proj, mlp.up_proj, mlp.down_proj, mlp.act_fn, mask\\n        )\\n\\n\\ndef print_mask_stats(name, masks):\\n    print(f\\\"\\\\nPer-layer sparsity ({name}):\\\")\\n    for idx in sorted(masks):\\n        sp = 1.0 - masks[idx].mean().item()\\n        print(f\\\"  Layer {idx:2d}: {sp:.3f}\\\")\\n    total_n = sum(m.numel() for m in masks.values())\\n    total_z = sum((m == 0).sum().item() for m in masks.values())\\n    sp = total_z / total_n\\n    print(f\\\"  Model-level: {sp:.3f}\\\")\\n    return sp\\n\\n\\ndef main():\\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--model_name_or_path\\\", type=str,\\n                        default=\\\"/root/distill_sparse_swiglu/models/llama-3.1-8b\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--predictor_path\\\", type=str, required=True)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--sparsity_target\\\", type=float, default=0.5)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--calibration_samples\\\", type=int, default=32)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--seq_len\\\", type=int, default=2048)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--tasks\\\", type=str, required=True,\\n                        help=\\\"Comma-separated lm-eval task names\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--num_fewshot\\\", type=int, default=None)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--limit\\\", type=int, default=None,\\n                        help=\\\"Limit samples per task (for dry-run)\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--batch_size\\\", type=str, default=\\\"auto\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--output_path\\\", type=str, default=None)\\n    args = parser.parse_args()\\n\\n    device = torch.device(\\\"cuda:0\\\")\\n\\n    print(f\\\"Loading model from {args.model_name_or_path} ...\\\")\\n    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(args.model_name_or_path)\\n    if tokenizer.pad_token is None:\\n        tokenizer.pad_token = tokenizer.eos_token\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.46}}"}

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Key findings so far:

1. **lm_eval_sparse.py** - Predictor-based masking (TEAL with predictor). Args:
   - `--model_name_or_path` (default: `/root/distill_sparse_swiglu/models/llama-3.1-8b`)
   - `--predictor_path` (required)
   - `--sparsity_target` (default: 0.5)
   - `--calibration_samples` (default: 32)
   - `--seq_len` (default: 2048)
   - `--tasks` (required, comma-separated)
   - `--num_fewshot` (default: None)
   - `--limit` (default: None)
   - `--batch_size` (default: "auto")
   - `--output_path`
   - Device is hardcoded to `cuda:0` with no GPU flag option

2. **lm_eval_teal.py** - Vanilla TEAL (training-free, activation-magnitude based) with the same arguments as the sparse version except it doesn't require a predictor path and includes a `--gpu` flag instead.

3. **Checkpoint structure** varies by method: KL and STE checkpoints store `predictor_kl_normalized.pt`, while BCE has `predictor_bce.pt` plus step checkpoints. I need to verify that the sparse loader's `ckpt["predictors"]` key works across all checkpoint types, and check whether the BCE and STE checkpoints follow the same structure.

4. **lm_eval_sparse.py also hardcodes cuda:0** without a GPU parameter, unlike TEAL. I should verify the checkpoint key structures, check if anything's currently running on the server, and look at the KL results to see if that run completed.
```

2. BCE checkpoint file is named `predictor_bce.pt` (not `predictor_kl_normalized.pt`) — the key inside might be different too.

   - **Issue**: Hardcoded `cuda:0`, no `--gpu` parameter
   - Checkpoint format: expects `ckpt["predictors"]` key
   - All conditions trained with `train.py` save with `"predictors"` key → compatible


2. **`lm_eval_teal.py`** — Training-free vanilla TEAL using activation magnitudes, no predictor required, and includes `--gpu` parameter support.
3. **`benchmark_eval.py`** — The most comprehensive option, supporting predictor-based modes with `--checkpoint` and `--sparsity_mode`, training-free baselines via `--baseline_mode`, and dense evaluation.

For the baseline mode, it has the standard few-shot settings built in and points to the correct model at `/root/autodl-tmp/models/llama-3.1-8b`. I'm verifying the checkpoints now—KL 50% is already done with the mvp_kl_norm_v2_s42 checkpoint, and KL 30% is currently running with kl_sparsity30_s42_v3, both using the predictor_kl_normalized.pt file which has the right key structure.

Looking at the remaining checkpoints to validate: KL 70%, STE 50%, and BCE iso-compute 50% all check out with their respective files and keys, while TEAL 50% and WINA 50% are training-free methods so they don't need checkpoint files. GPU 0 is actively running the KL 30% sparsity experiment right now.

The main blocker is that `lm_eval_sparse.py` hardcodes CUDA device 0, which prevents running multiple predictor-based conditions in parallel—I could either switch to `benchmark_eval.py` which has a `--gpu` flag, add an environment variable workaround, or modify the script directly to accept a GPU argument. WINA also lacks its own evaluation script, but `benchmark_eval.py` already supports it via the `--baseline_mode wina` flag, so that's covered.

There's a potential consistency issue with few-shot settings: `lm_eval_sparse.py` doesn't explicitly set `--num_fewshot`, so it defaults to None and relies on task-level YAML configs, which should match what `benchmark_eval.py` does with its explicit defaults. Once the KL 30% and TEAL 50% runs finish in about 50 minutes each, I'll move forward with the remaining conditions. based on the KL 50% timing of 3010s):

**Priority 3-6 (can run 2 at a time on GPU 0 + GPU 1):**

| # | Condition | Script | Command | Est. Time |
|---|-----------|--------|---------|-----------|
| 3 | KL 70% | benchmark_eval.py or lm_eval_sparse.py | see below | ~50 min |
| 4 | STE 50% | benchmark_eval.py or lm_eval_sparse.py | see below | ~50 min |
| 5 | BCE 50% | benchmark_eval.py or lm_eval_sparse.py | see below | ~50 min |
| 6 | WINA 50% | benchmark_eval.py | see below | ~50 min |

I need to reconsider the GPU allocation — while each model takes 16-20 GB and the RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell has ~98GB, the auto batch sizing in lm-eval could be memory-intensive, so I'll stick with one job per GPU to be safe. This gives me three sequential rounds: KL 30% and TEAL 50% running now, then KL 70% paired with STE 50%, and finally BCE 50% with WINA 50%, each round taking about 50 minutes.

Now I'm deciding between `benchmark_eval.py` and `lm_eval_sparse.py`. The benchmark script is more complete with GPU support, but I need to verify they produce consistent results with the existing KL 50% baseline. Looking at how they handle the predictor and few-shot configuration, they're largely equivalent in their core evaluation logic, though they differ slightly in how defaults are specified. which lets lm-eval use its own defaults from task YAML)

Let me check if these are the same. In lm-eval, tasks like arc_challenge, winogrande, mmlu, gsm8k have built-in defaults for num_fewshot in their YAML config files. The standard practice is:
- ARC-c: 25-shot
- WinoGrande: 5-shot
- MMLU: 5-shot
- GSM8K: 5-shot (some use 8-shot)

benchmark_eval.py uses:
- ARC-c: 25-shot
- WinoGrande: 5-shot
- MMLU: 5-shot
- GSM8K: 8-shot

So for GSM8K there could be a difference if lm-eval's default is 5-shot. But this is a minor concern.

I think the best path forward is to stick with `benchmark_eval.py` for the remaining conditions since it has GPU support and proper structure, though if I need perfect alignment with the existing KL 50% results, I could use `lm_eval_sparse.py` with `CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=1` for parallel runs.

## 代码审查结果

### 现有脚本

| 脚本 | 用途 | `--gpu` | 适用条件 |
|------|------|---------|---------|
| `lm_eval_sparse.py` | 加载 predictor → TEAL global mask → lm-eval | **无（硬编码 cuda:0）** | KL/STE/BCE（需要 predictor） |
| `lm_eval_teal.py` | 训练无关的 activation-magnitude masking → lm-eval | ✅ | vanilla TEAL |
| `benchmark_eval.py` | 通用脚本，支持所有模式 | ✅ | 全部（predictor / baseline / dense） |

### Checkpoint 验证

全部存在且 key 兼容（`ckpt["predictors"]`）：

| Condition | Checkpoint 路径 | 文件名 |
|-----------|---------------|--------|
| KL 50% (已完成) | `checkpoints/mvp_kl_norm_v2_s42/` | `predictor_kl_normalized.pt` |
| KL 30% | `checkpoints/kl_sparsity30_s42_v3/` | `predictor_kl_normalized.pt` |
| KL 70% | `checkpoints/kl_sparsity70_s42/` | `predictor_kl_normalized.pt` |
| STE 50% | `checkpoints/ste_baseline_s42/` | `predictor_kl_normalized.pt` |
| BCE iso-compute 50% | `checkpoints/bce_isocompute_v2_s42/` | `predictor_bce.pt` |
| TEAL 50% | 无需 checkpoint | — |
| WINA 50% | 无需 checkpoint | — |

### 当前 GPU 状态

- **GPU 0** (PID 191340): `lm_eval_sparse.py` — **KL 30%** 正在跑 → `results/lm_eval_kl_30pct`
- **GPU 1** (PID 190835): `lm_eval_teal.py` — **TEAL 50%** 正在跑 → `results/lm_eval_teal_50pct`

### 关键问题

1. **`lm_eval_sparse.py` 硬编码 `cuda:0`** — 无法利用双 GPU 并行跑两个 predictor 条件。解决方案：后续条件改用 `benchmark_eval.py`（有 `--gpu` 参数），或加 `CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES` 环境变量。
2. **WINA 无专用 lm-eval 脚本** — `benchmark_eval.py --baseline_mode wina` 可直接支持。
3. **few-shot 一致性**：`lm_eval_sparse.py` 的 `--num_fewshot` 默认 None（用 lm-eval task 自带默认值），`benchmark_eval.py` 显式设置了 ARC-c=25, WG=5, MMLU=5, GSM8K=8。GSM8K 可能有差异（lm-eval 默认可能是 5-shot vs benchmark_eval 的 8-shot）。建议统一使用 `benchmark_eval.py`。

---

## 执行计划

推荐统一用 `benchmark_eval.py`（`--sparsity_mode teal_global`），有 `--gpu` 支持且标准化 few-shot。

| GPU | 条件 | 状态 |
|-----|------|------|
| 0 | KL 30% | 🔄 运行中 |
| 1 | TEAL 50% | 🔄 运行中 |

### Round 2（Round 1 完成后）

```bash
# KL 70% — GPU 0
cd /root/distill_sparse_swiglu && source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && \
CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0 nohup python -u src/benchmark_eval.py \
  --exp_id kl_70pct_s42 \
  --checkpoint checkpoints/kl_sparsity70_s42/predictor_kl_normalized.pt \
  --sparsity_mode teal_global \
  --sparsity_target 0.7 \
  --tasks arc_challenge,winogrande,mmlu,gsm8k \
  --gpu 0 \
  > results/lm_eval_kl_70pct.log 2>&1 &

# STE 50% — GPU 1
CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=1 nohup python -u src/benchmark_eval.py \
  --exp_id ste_50pct_s42 \
  --checkpoint checkpoints/ste_baseline_s42/predictor_kl_normalized.pt \
  --sparsity_mode teal_global \
  --sparsity_target 0.5 \
  --tasks arc_challenge,winogrande,mmlu,gsm8k \
  --gpu 0 \
  > results/lm_eval_ste_50pct.log 2>&1 &
```

### Round 3（Round 2 完成后）

```bash

CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0 nohup python -u src/benchmark_eval.py \
  --exp_id bce_isocompute_50pct_s42 \
  --checkpoint checkpoints/bce_isocompute_v2_s42/predictor_bce.pt \
  --sparsity_mode teal_global \
  --sparsity_target 0.5 \
  --tasks arc_challenge,winogrande,mmlu,gsm8k \

# WINA 50% — GPU 1
CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=1 nohup python -u src/benchmark_eval.py \
  --exp_id wina_50pct \
  --baseline_mode wina \
  --sparsity_target 0.5 \
  --tasks arc_challenge,winogrande,mmlu,gsm8k \
  --gpu 0 \
  > results/lm_eval_wina_50pct.log 2>&1 &
```

| # | 条件 | 脚本 | 关键参数 | Checkpoint | 预计时间 |
|---|------|------|---------|-----------|---------|
| 1 | KL 30% | lm_eval_sparse.py | `--sparsity_target 0.3` | `kl_sparsity30_s42_v3/predictor_kl_normalized.pt` | ~50 min (运行中) |

| 5 | BCE 50% | benchmark_eval.py | `--sparsity_mode teal_global --sparsity_target 0.5` | `bce_isocompute_v2_s42/predictor_bce.pt` | ~50 min |
| 6 | WINA 50% | benchmark_eval.py | `--baseline_mode wina --sparsity_target 0.5` | 无需 | ~50 min |
